## Implementation boundaries and remaining scope

Vibration requires real grounded source/receiver contact through touching collision components, including carpet/slabs/fences. Air gaps and point/edge contacts do not conduct. Source cushioning, interface transfer, bulk damping and receiver coupling are distinct authored approximations. The bounded solver reports the energy of a validated path above threshold, which can understate the strongest route. It omits elastic modes, phase, resonance, summed multipath and calibrated mechanical travel time. Crouching reduces footsteps only; accepted tool progress retains tool strength. Incomplete searches remain UNKNOWN, never proof of silence.

Received vibration stores only the receiver region with24-block uncertainty, no identity, source range or destination. It cannot authorize digging or melee. Separate actionable hearing/echo takes priority. Solver contacts/path belong to diagnostics only. Repeated last-result frames are not new impulses; capture completeness is separate from sensing success.

The direct-return echo slice is implemented and verified above. Indirect echoes, revised hearing/transmission and external masking remain later stages. Keep the approved leather helmet and all nine approved runtime clips unchanged; source provenance is under `tools/audio_sources/blind_architect/` and `AUDIO_NOTICE.md`. No new dependency or research asset was imported. The owner performs visual playtests; never automatically reset, replay or advance a visible encounter merely to collect evidence.
